[\[298a.1\]]kun gyi 'byung gnas kyis/_/rgya mtsho rgyas pa ji bzhin du/_/rang byung ye shes de bzhin te/_mi zad skad cig rab mkhyen pa/_/mgon po'i ye shes shin tu zab/_/tshad med de bzhin bgrang du [{]myed[,]med[}]/_de bas sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul pa/_/
[\[298a.2\]]bsam gyis [{]myi [,]mi [}]khyab tshad med ston/_/sgyu ma'i mtshan nyid ston pa yi/_/sgyu mkhan mkhas pa ji lta ba/_/de ltar sangs rgyas mkhyen dbang yang /_/rnam par 'phrul pa rab tu ston/_/yid bzhin nor bu rnam dag pa/_/bsam don rdzogs byed ji lta ba/_/de bzhin 
[\[298a.3\]]bsam pa dag pa la/_/rgyal ba smon lam rdzogs pa mdzad/_/'od snang gsal ba byed pa yi/_/rin chen rnam snang ji lta ba/_/de ltar thams cad mkhyen pa dag_/'gro ba'i bsam pa gsal bar mdzad/_/ji ltar phyogs la mngon lta ba'i/_/rin chen 
[\[298a.4\]]zur brgyad rab gnas pa/_de bzhin chags med sgron ma yang /_/chos dbyings rab tu snang ba mdzad du/_/chu 'dang rin chen 'od gsal ba/_/ji ltar dri med chu 'dang byed/_/de bzhin sangs rgyas mthong bas kyang /_/'gro ba'i dbang po dag par 'gyur/_/de nas 
[\[298a.5\]]byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi dbyings su smon lam rab tu 'phrul ba'i zla ba'i rgyal pos/_/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/_/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so/_/phyogs 'di in dra nI la yi/_/kha dog 
[\[298a.6\]]mdog gcig byas pa ltar/_sangs rgyas mthong bas skye dgu rnams/_/de bzhin byang chub mdog tu 'gyur/_/sangs rgyas rdul phran re re la/_/rnam par 'phrul pa sna tshogs rnams/_/tshad kyis gzhal du med pa ston/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnam dag 
[\[298a.7\]]mdzad/_/de ni rmad byung shin tu zab/_/mtha' yas bsgrub par dka' ba ste/_/blo ldan ye shes yul de la/_/'jig rten rnams ni 'bab mi nus/_/byang chub sems dpa' bsgrub don du/_/bkod pa phun sum tshogs pa dang /_/sangs rgyas tshul 
[\[298b\]]
[\[298b.1\]]gyis rnam sbyangs pa'i/_/chos kyi dbyings la 'jug pa ston/_/sangs rgyas zhing rnams bsam yas pa/_/blo ldan bskor ba'i sangs rgyas kyis/_/kun nas gang bar gyur pa yang /_/rgyal ba de la ston pa mdzad/_/chos kun mnga' sgyur ston pa ste/_/shAkya'i 
[\[298b.2\]]khyu mchog skyes par gyur/_/de yi cho 'phrul tshad med pa/_'di dag rab tu byung ba yin/_/brtan pa'i spyod pa sna tshogs rnams/_/tshad med rnam par gzigs gyur nas/_/rnam par 'phrul pa mtha' yas pa/_/tshad med gzi can ston pa mdzad/_/'jig 
[\[298b.3\]]rten mgon po rgyal sras rnams/_/chos kyi dbyings la slob pa mdzad/_/chos rnams thams cad yongs la yang /_/chags med ye shes spyod yul 'gyur/_/skyes bu dbang po'i byin rlabs kyis/_/cho 'phrul brgya dang rab ldan zhing /_/'jig rten thams cad 
[\[298b.4\]]rnam spyod pa'i/_/chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu bskor/_/sems can gtso bo'i yul la yang /_/ye shes dkyil 'khor yongs sbyongs shing /_/shes rab chen po'i klu chen rnams/_/'jig rten thams cad 'grel bar 'gyur/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[298b.5\]]chos kyi don gyi blo gros gzi brjid rgyal pos/_/bcom ldan 'das kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/_de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so/_/drang srong dam pa'i mchog rnams kyi/_/dus gsum nyan thos gang byung 
[\[298b.6\]]ba/_/de dag kun gyis bde gshegs kyi/_/gshegs thabs 'jog 'deg mi shes so/_/dus gsum ma lus thams cad kyi/_/ma nor rang rgyal gang byung ba/_/de dag gis kyang mgon po yi/_/gshegs thabs 'jog 'deg mi rig go_/
[\[298b.7\]]ma rig mun pas bsgribs pa rnams/_/khyi ltar ltong la rgyus pa yi/_/sems can de 'dra kun gyis ni/_/mgon po [{]myi [,]mi [}]shes smos mi dgos/_/rgyal ba tshad mas gzhal ba [{]myin[,]min[}]/_/de ni shes par mi nus te/_/sangs rgyas chags med ye shes can/_/
[\[299a\]]
[\[299a.1\]]tshig gi lam las shin tu 'das/_/zla ba nya rgyas 'od mnga' ba/_brtan pa mtshan gyis rnam par bris/_/de ni bskal pa dpag yas par/_/rnam 'phrul thogs med spyod pa mdzad/_/tshul sgo re res sangs rgyas la/_/shin tu mnyam 
[\[299a.2\]]bzhag bsam byas kyang /_/bskal pa bye ba bsam yas pa/_/brjod du med pa zad 'gyur gyi/_/rang byung rnams kyi yon tan las/_/phyogs gcig cha yi mthar [{]myi [,]mi [}]phyin/_/sangs rgyas de yi zhal bltas kyang /_/sangs rgyas chos ni bsam mi khyab/_/gang zhig de la smon 
[\[299a.3\]]byed cing /_/gang zhig yid kyis dga' mos pa/_/de dag kun gyi de 'dra ba'i/_/spyod yul blta dka' 'thob par 'gyur/_/bsod nams ye shes mtha' yas shing /_/tshogs can yangs pas rnam gnon la/_/dri med blo ldan sems gnas pa/_/de dag 'di yi tshul la 
[\[299a.4\]]'jug_/de dag smon lam rgya che bas/_/sems dang sdom pa rgya che ste/_/rgyal ba'i spyod yul thob gyur cing /_/byang chub rgya chen gnon par 'gyur/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' bdud kyi dkyil 'khor rnam par 'thor ba'i ye shes rgyal mtshan gyi rgyal pos/_
[\[299a.5\]]sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/_/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so/_/chags med ye shes sku mnga' bas/_/rang byung rnams ni sku med de/_/bsam yas ye shes yul mnga' bas/_/de dag bsam par mi 
[\[299a.6\]]nus so/_/phrin las dkar po bsam yas pas/_/sangs rgyas sku rab bsgrub pa'i phyir/_/de la 'jig rten gsum mi 'go_/mtshan dang dpe byad bzang pos mdzes/_/chos kyi dbyings kyis rnam sbyangs pas/_/'jig rten kun tu snang ba mdzad/_/sangs 
[\[299a.7\]]rgyas byang chub sgo yin te/_/ye shes kun gyi gnas chen no/_/chags pa thams cad rnam spangs pas/_/rdul med spros pa mi mnga'o/_/ye shes 'od zer rab 'gyed pas/_'jig rten kun tu nyi ma 'dra/_/khams gsum thams cad rnam sbyong 
[\[299b\]]
[\[299b.1\]]pas/_/srid pa'i 'jigs pa rnam par gcod/_/byang chub sems dpa' bsgrub pa mdzad/_/byang chub 'byung gnas de bzhin no/_/kha dog mtha' yas ston mdzad kyang /_/kha dog kun la gnas pa med/_/sems can kun gyis bsam yas pa'i/_/kha dog 
[\[299b.2\]]tshad med yongs su ston/_/skad cig gcig la sangs rgyas kyi/_/byang chub bsam yas sbyangs pas na/_/de yi ye shes mthar phyin par/_/rtag par sus kyang mi nus so/_/ye shes bstan pa zad [{]myi [,]mi [}]shes/_/rang bzhin rnam par 'gyur ba med/_/de la 
[\[299b.3\]]dus gsum rgyal ba kun/_/skad cig gcig gis rab tu brjod/_/rtag par las gzhan yongs btang ste/_/shes ldan byang chub don gnyer bas/_/sems la sems shes bya ba yang /_de la sems skye med par bsam/_/gang gi sangs rgyas 
[\[299b.4\]]bskyed pa yi/_/sangs rgyas chos rnams bsam mi khyab/_/de dag brjod pa'i yul ma yin/_/shin tu zab pas tshig lam spangs/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' rnam par snang mdzad kyi smon lam dpal gyi rgyal mtshan gyis/_sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi 
[\[299b.5\]]rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par blta/_de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so/_/dran pa rmongs med dag pa rnams/_/chos 'dzin shin tu nges rtogs shing /_/blo gros bsam gyis mi khyab la/_/byang chub rgya mtsho [{]myi [,]mi [}]zad pa/_/de dag yid ni 'di 
[\[299b.6\]]la des/_/de dag spyod pa'i spyod yul yin/_/de dag shes pa 'dir [{]myi [,]mi [}]g.yo/_/de dag 'di la the tsom 'chad/_/de dag skyo bar [{]myi [,]mi [}]'gyur te/_/de dag yid kyang zhum mi 'gyur/_/sangs rgyas chos la rab gzhol bar/_/de dag sems kyang rab tu 'byung /_
[\[299b.7\]]de dag dad pa rting tshugs shing /_/yang dag 'phags pa rab tu bskyed/_/dmigs med gnas pa med pa yi/_/ye shes 'di la de dag dga'/_/bskal pa bye bar bstsags pa yi/_/yongs su rdzogs pa'i dge ba'i chos/_/ye shes 'di 'dod mtshungs 
[\[300a\]]
[\[300a.1\]]pa med/_/de dag thams cad yongs su sngo/_/'khor ba'i nang na rnam rgyu yang /_/'khor ba dag la gnas pa [{]myin[,]min[}]/_/sangs rgyas chos rnams rab rtogs pas/_/sangs rgyas yul la rab tu rtse/_/sems can khams ni ji snyed 
[\[300a.2\]]du/_/'jig rten phun sum tshogs rgyu ba/_/de dag thams cad brtan pas spangs/_/sangs rgyas phun sum tshogs la gnas/_/sangs rgyas rtag tu 'byung 'gyur kyang /_'jig rten don med rab tu gnas/_/chags med spyod pa de dag ni/_/rtag tu sems 
[\[300a.3\]]can don la gnas/_/de dag spyod pa mtshungs med de/_/lus can kun gyis bsam mi khyab/_/'jig rten bde ba kun sems pas/_/de dag sdug bsngal rnam par bzlog_/'jig rten kun la snying brtse zhing /_/byang chub ye shes rnam dag pas/_/de dag 
[\[300a.4\]]'jig rten snang bar gyur/_/'jig rten thams cad rab tu 'grel/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' sgrib pa thams cad rnam par 'thor ba'i rgyal pos/_/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/_de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di 
[\[300a.5\]]dag smras so/_/bskal pa bye ba phrag brgyar yang /_/sangs rgyas sgra ni rab dkon na/_/the tsom thams cad gcod pa yi/_/dam pa mthong ba ci zhig smos/_/chos kun thugs su chud rtogs pa/_/'jig rten gsum po'i bsod nams stegs/_/sems 
[\[300a.6\]]can thams cad rnam sbyong ba'i/_/'jig rten sgron ma mthong gis dga'/_/sems can gtso bo ma smad pa'i/_/gzugs kyi sku ni mthong gyur na/_/bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig tu/_/ngoms shing tshim pa skyer mi btub/_/[{]myi'i [,]mi'i [}]dbang po'i sku gzugs la/_/rgyal 
[\[300a.7\]]ba'i sras rnams lta lta zhing /_/rang gi dge ba'i chags med pa/_/gzhan don brtson pa byang chub bsngo/_/thub pa chen po'i gzugs kyi sku/_/de las so so yang dag rig_/chags med ye shes 'byung 'gyur bas/_/de ni sangs rgyas byang chub sgo/_/sems 
[\[300b\]]
[\[300b.1\]]can bsam yas tshad [{]myed [,]med [}]pa/_/snang bar mdzad de theg chen la/_/btsud nas thub pa chen po'i mchog_/byang chub dam par lung yang ston/_/bsod nams chen po'i rang bzhin zhing /_/ye shes dkyil 'khor shar cing 'byung /_/'jig rten tshad med kun tu snang /_/

[\[300b.6\]]can don la brtson mdzad pa'i/_/sangs rgyas rnams la lus can rnams/_/kun gyis lan glan cis kyang nus/_/ngan song gsum po mi bzad par/_/bskal pa bye bar btsos bla yi/_/chags pa thams cad zlog mdzad pa'i/_/ston pa mi mthong ma 
[\[300b.7\]]yin no/_/sems can kun gyi rgyud rnams na/_/sdug bsngal phung po ji yod pa/_/ma lus nyams su bzod nus kyi/_/sangs rgyas mi mthong ma yin no/_/'jig rten 'di dag thams cad na/_/tha dad ngan song rgyud yod pa/_/de dag yun ring gnas 
[\[301a\]]
[\[301a.1\]]bla yi/_/sangs rgyas mi mthong ma yin no/_/sems can dmyal ba re rer yang /_/bskal pa tshad med gnas bla yi/_/mtha' yas rgyal bas brnyes pa yi/_/byang chub mchog las ring gnas [{]myin[,]min[}]/_/ci zhig phyir ni ngan song 
[\[301a.2\]]du/_/yun ring gnas par 'dod ce na/_/rgyal ba'i dbang po mthong ba ni/_/ye shes 'phel ba'i rgyur 'gyur phyir/_/rgyal ba 'jig rten mchog mthong na/_/sdug bsngal thams cad 'chad par 'gyur/_/rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas spyod yul gyi/_/ye shes

[\[301a.4\]]'gyur/_/'jig rten 'das dang 'jig rten gyi/_/bsam pa'ang yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang pos byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs thams cad la rnam par bltas te/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i ting 
[\[301a.5\]]nge 'dzin seng ge rnam par bsgyings pa 'di nyid de bas kyang rnam par dbye ba dang rgya cher brjod pa dang /_gsal bar bya ba dang rab tu brjod pa dang /_yang dag par bstan pa dang /_shin tu snang bar bya ba dang /_rab tu gsal bar bya ba dang man ngag bstan pa'i phyir/_chos kyi dbyings 
[\[301a.6\]]kyi tshul nam mkha'i dbyings dang mnyam pas/_dus gsum mnyam pa nyid dang /_chos kyi dbyings mnyam pa nyid dang sems can gyi khams mnyam pa nyid dang /_'jig rten thams cad mnyam pa nyid dang las kyi rgyud thams cad mnyam pa nyid dang sems can gyi bsam 
[\[301a.7\]]pa mnyam pa nyid dang /_sems can gyi mos pa mnyam pa nyid dang /_chos kyi snang ba mnyam pa nyid dang /_sems can yongs su smin par bya ba'i dus mnyam pa nyid dang /_'gro ba thams cad kyi dbang po mnyam pa nyid kyis/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i ting nge 'dzin seng

[\[301b.1\]]ge rnam par bsgyings pa/_'di nyid byang chub sems dpa' de dag la bstan pa'i tshul bcus yang dag par rab tu ston te/_bcu gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_chos kyi dbyings thams cad du gtogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba rnams la/_/sangs rgyas gcig

[\[301b.7\]]gsum gyi sangs rgyas kyi smon lam rgya mtsho thams cad ba spu'i sgo thams cad nas rab tu brjod pa phyi ma'i mthar byin gyis brlabs pas/_byang chub sems dpa' yang dag par 'byung bar bstan pa dang /_'di lta ste/_chos kyi dbyings dang mnyam pa'i seng ge'i khri dang /_
[\[302a\]]
[\[302a.1\]]byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor dang /_snying po byang chub kyi rgyan tha [{]myi [,]mi [}]dad pa dang /_chos kyi 'khor lo rnam pa tha dad pa rab tu bskor ba/_phyi ma'i mthar byin gyis brlab pa bstan pa'o/_/kye rgyal ba'i sras de ltar seng ge rnam 
[\[302a.2\]]par bsgyings pa'i ting nge 'dzin 'di la bstan pa bcu po de dag la [{]stsogs [,]sogs [}]pa shin tu bstan pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed yod de/_kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes kyi spyod yul lo/_/de nas byang chub sems 
[\[302a.3\]]dpa' kun tu bzang pos de bzhin gshegs pa'i ting nge 'dzin chen po seng ge rnam par bsgyings pa 'di nyid kyi don gyi phyogs bstan pa'i phyir sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis de bzhin gshegs pa'i zhal du lta zhing 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho thams cad la

[\[302a.5\]]bltas nas ye shes bsam gyis [{]myi [,]mi [}]khyab pa'i sgyu ma'i chos nyid la rnam par bltas te/_dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas mnyam pa nyid bsam gyis mi khyab pa la rnam par bltas nas/_mtha' dang dbus med pa'i ngag gi lam dang nges pa'i tshig dang /_chos kyi 
[\[302a.6\]]tshul thams cad la rnam par bltas te/_de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so/_/ba spu gcig la rgya mtsho rgal ba'i zhing /_/zhing kun shin tu phra ba'i rdul dang mnyam/_/de nas sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor/_kun gyis yongs 
[\[302a.7\]]bskor sangs rgyas gdan la bzhugs/_/spu gcig rtse la zhing mang rgya mtsho rnams/_/snying po byang chub pad+mo'i gdan gnas pas/_/chos dbyings rgya chen yongs su rgyas bkang zhing /_/shing drung dam pa kun na mgon po snang /_/zhing rnams kun gyi rdul

[\[302b.1\]]phran rab mang rnyed/_/rgyal ba spu rtse gcig la yongs bzhugs pa/_/byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi yongs su bskor/_/kun kyang bzang po'i spyod pa ston pa mdzad/_/zhing mchog gcig na rgyal ba bzhugs gyur kyang /_/byang chub sems dpa'i sprin mang mi zad pa/_de

[\[302b.3\]]kun kyang 'khor nas ldang /_/zhing rnams kun tu gzugs brnyan rab tu ston/_/chos kyi rgyal po rgyal ba'i ye shes mtsho/_/de dag kun kyang bzang po'i spyod la gnas/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi 'khor du nye bar 'dong /_/'byam klas zhing rnams kun na 'khod spyod cing /_/
[\[302b.4\]]byang chub sems dpa'i dga' ba rab gnas te/_/chos kyi sprin mnyan dpa' bo de dag ni/_/zhing gcig du yang bskal pa bye bar spyod/_/byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod par byed/_/chos kyi gang chen mtsho spyod rnam par snang /_/smon lam rgya mtsho kun la rab tu 
[\[302b.5\]]'jug_/de dag sangs rgyas sa yi spyod yul gnas/_/rgyal ba'i chos rnams gzhan dang gzhan byung ba'i/_/de dag kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa shes/_/sangs rgyas yon tan legs pa rgya mtsho dang /_/rnam par 'phrul pa rgya chen yangs la 'jug_/bde 
[\[302b.6\]]gshegs chos dbyings kun tu rgyas bkang zhing /_/zhing rnams kun gyi rdul phran ji snyed pa'i/_/sku yi sprin rnams rtag tu 'gyed mdzad cing /_/chos kyi char gyis byang chub rab la dgod//_//sangs rgyas rmad gcad ces bya ba shin tu rgyas pa chen

[\[303a\]]
[\[303a.1\]]kyi dbyings sgo kun nas rnam par dmigs pas dus gsum snang ba zhes bya ba'i 'od zer chen po 'od zer gyi 'khor sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dang ldan pa bkye ste/_phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams 
[\[303a.2\]]rgya mtsho thams cad kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'byam klas pa thams cad snang bar mdzad do/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' dze ta'i tshal du tshogs pa de dag gi chos kyi dbyings thams cad du gtogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mtha' klas pa 
[\[303a.3\]]thams cad dang /_/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba'i khongs su chud pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba dag la/_sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi [{]mying [,]ming [}]du bya ba kha dog sna tshogs dang /_rnam pa sna tshogs su rnam par dag pa dang /_zhing

[\[303b.1\]]'khor dag na rnam par 'phrul pa sna tshogs dang /_spyod lam sna tshogs dang /_lus sna tshogs dang /_ting nge 'dzin gyi sgo rnam par [{]dmyigs [,]dmigs [}]pa sna tshogs dang /_ting nge 'dzin gyi mngon par shes pa sna tshogs dang /_rigs dang rgyud sna tshogs su byung ba dang /_kha dog

[\[304a.1\]]bya ba'i phyogs dang /_las kyi 'du byed sna tshogs kyis yongs su grub pa'i phyogs dang /_skra'i rtse mo gcig la mtha' dang dbus med pa'i nam mkha'i dbyings kyi [{]mying [,]ming [}]du byar gtogs pa'i phyogs kyi khongs su chud pa'i dus gsum gyi dbyings

[\[304b.1\]]pa yin te/_/la la ni chos kyi sku la 'jug go_/la la ni gzugs kyi sku la 'jug go_/la la ni sngon gyi byang chub sems dpa'i yongs su bsgrub pa la 'jug go_/la la ni pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs pa la 'jug go_/la la ni spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor yongs su dag pa'i

[\[305a.1\]]bcu 'thob pas rnam par 'byed pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin sangs rgyas kyi bsnyengs pa mi mnga' ba'i rgyan gyis rnam par gnon cing rnam par bsgyings pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin chos kyi dbyings kyi tshul thams cad

[\[305b.1\]]thams cad du khyad par du 'phags pa'i dkyil 'khor chags pa med pa'i rnam par spyod pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad skyed pa las byung ba'i byin gyi rlabs dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin yon tan rgya mtsho

[\[306b.1\]]dpa'i ting nge 'dzin sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rgyal mtshan byin gyi rlabs kyis chos ston pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin dus gsum gyi mu la mthar thug pa med pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin bskal pa thams cad khong du chud pas tha [{]myi [,]mi [}]dad pa'i ye shes dang /_

[\[307a.1\]]'dzin bsod nams mi zad pa'i snying po dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin sangs rgyas mtha' yas pa mthong ba'i yul rab tu snang ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin chos thams cad la rdo rje'i seng ge ltar rab tu

[\[322a.1\]]bzhag gsol/_/sems can thams cad 'dul las mi ldog pa/_/bsam gtan ting 'dzin kha khyer mthor sgron cing /_/shes rab thabs gnyis 'byor mthun 'gro rgyu ba'i/_/chos kyi theg pa'i mchog la bdag bzhag gsol/_/
[\[322a.2\]]smon lam 'khor los lam gyi 'khor sbyong ba/_/chos kyi gzungs dam mthu brtan che ldan zhing /_/ye shes 'khrul 'khor legs sbyangs legs byas pa'i/_/chos kyi theg pa de la bdag skyon gsol/_/kun tu bzang po'i spyod pas rab sbyangs shing /_/sems 
[\[322a.3\]]can lta bas rgyu la thogs med pa/_/kun tu bzang po'i spyod pas gnon gyur pa'i/_/ye shes theg pas bdag la bsdud du gsol/_/de ni brtan pas rdo rje bzhin gnas pa/_/ye shes sgyu mas legs sbyangs legs byas shing /_/sgrib dang chags 
[\[322a.4\]]pa thams cad gcod pa yi/_/bzang po'i theg la bdag kyang bskyon du gsol/_/'gro mnyam dri med shin tu yangs gyur pa/_/sems can thams cad skyob pas bde sgrub cing /_/chos dbyings shin tu rgya chen rnam snang ba'i/_/byang chub theg 
[\[322a.5\]]la bdag kyang bskyon du gsol/_/sdug bsngal phung po rab 'byung gcod byed pa/_/nyon mongs las kyi rdul mang sbyong sel cing /_/bdud dang phas kyi rgol ba kun 'joms pa'i/_/chos kyi theg la bdag kyang bskyon du gsol/_/phyogs rnams kun tu ye shes 
[\[322a.6\]]rab snang ba/_/chos dbyings nam mkha' rnam par klubs byed cing /_/sems can kun gyi bsam pa rdzogs byed pa'i/_/chos kyi theg la bdag kyang bskyon du gsol/_/rnam dag mkha' ltar tshad med mi zad pa/_/sred dang ma rig lta ba'i skyon med cing /_/
[\[322a.7\]]sems can kun la phan par rab gnas pa'i/_/chos kyi theg la bdag kyang bskyon du gsol/_/rlung chen langs pa'i shugs kyi shugs ldan pas/_/smon lam mthu yis 'jig rten kun 'dzin cing /_/zhi ba'i gnas rab kun sar skyel byed pa'i/_/
[\[322b\]]
[\[322b.1\]]chos kyi theg la bdag kyang bskyon du gsol/_/sa chen bzhi yi stobs kyi stobs ldan pa/_/snying rje'i mthu dang shugs kyis khur khyer zhing /_/ye shes lo tog 'gro ba nyer 'tsho ba'i/_/theg pa'i mchog la bdag kyang bskyon du gsol/_/nyi ma lta bur 
[\[322b.2\]]'gro ba nyer 'tsho zhing /_/bsdu ba rab yangs 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor can/_/gzungs kyi stobs mchog dag pa'i 'od bzang ba'i/_/ye shes nyi ma bdag la bstan tu gsol/_/bskal pa mang po du mar rab bslabs pas/_rgyu yi tshul dang su kun 
[\[322b.3\]]mkhas gyur pa/_/gang gis 'dus byas grong khyer gzhom 'gyur ba'i/_/shes brtan rdo rje 'phags pas bdag la stsal du gsol/_/sangs rgyas legs kun bslab pa phun sum gyis/_/ye shes rgya mtsho yangs pa gang zhig la/_/tshad med rgya 
[\[322b.4\]]mtsho'i blo mang bslab gyur pas/_/de legs ci 'dra 'phags pas bka' stsal gsol/_/gang na yang dag spyan mnga' khyod bzhugs shing /_/ye shes rgyal po'i cod pan legs brgyan te/_/chos kyi dbu thod dam pas dbu bcings pa'i/_/chos kyi 
[\[322b.5\]]rgyal po'i pho brang bdag kyang ston/_de nas 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pas glang po'i lta stangs kyis bltas te/_tshong dpon gyi bu nor [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]la 'di skad ces smras so/_/rigs kyi bu de ltar khyod kyis bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu 
[\[322b.6\]]sems bskyed de/_dge ba'i bshes gnyen gyi rjes su 'brang zhing byang chub sems dpa'i lam yongs su rdzogs par bya bar 'dod de/_byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa yongs su dri bar bsam pa legs so legs so/_/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_
[\[322b.7\]]dge ba'i bshes gnyen rnams la bsnyen cing bsten pa dang /_rim gror bya ba 'di ni thams cad mkhyen pa nyid yongs su grub par 'gyur ba'i gzhi yin te 'di ni mthun pa'i rgyu yin gyis/_rigs kyi bu de bas na khyod kyis dge ba'i bshes bsnyen 
[\[323a\]]
[\[323a.1\]]bkur bas yongs su ma skyo bar gyis shig_/nor [{]bzangs kyis [,]bzang gis [}]smras pa/_'phags pa ji ltar byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la bslab par bgyi/_ji ltar nan tan du bgyi/_ji ltar byang chub sems dpas byang 
[\[323a.2\]]chub sems dpa'i spyod pa brtsam par bgyi/_ji ltar byang chub sems dpas byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la spyad par bgyi/_ji ltar byang chub sems dpas byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa yongs su rdzogs par bgyi/_ji ltar byang chub sems dpas byang chub sems 
[\[323a.3\]]dpa'i spyod pa yongs su sbyang bar bgyi/_ji ltar byang chub sems dpas byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la 'jug pa bgyi/_ji ltar byang chub sems dpas byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa mngon par bsgrub par bgyi/_ji ltar byang chub sems dpas byang chub sems dpa'i 
[\[323a.4\]]spyod pa'i rjes su 'brang bar bgyi/_ji ltar byang chub sems dpas byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la lhag par dmigs par bgyi/_/ji ltar byang chub sems dpas byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa rgyas par bgyi/_/ji ltar byang chub sems dpas kun tu 
[\[323a.5\]]bzang po'i spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ba rgya cher bstan du gsol/_de nas 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pas tshong dpon gyi bu nor [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]la tshigs su bcad de smras pa/_/dge ba'i bsod nams gang chen mtsho/_/de ltar khyod ni nga 
[\[323a.6\]]yi drung du nyer phyin te/_/snying rje snying brtse rgya chen gyi/_/yid ldan byang chub dam pa tshol ba legs/_/'gro ba thams cad dgrol don du/_/spyod pa mtshungs med don du smon lam rab 'debs shing /_/'gro ba kun gyi skyabs 'gyur ba/_/byang chub sems 
[\[323a.7\]]dpa'i spyod pa tshul 'di yin/_/byang chub sems dpa'i brtan pa rnams/_/'khor ba'i nang na skyo med yid kyis spyod byed pa/_/de dag kun tu bzang po yi/_/spyod pa thub med chags med 'thob par 'gyur/_/bsod nams 'od can bsod nams 
[\[323b\]]
[\[323b.1\]]dpal/_/de ltar khyod ni kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa la/_/'gro la phan phyir smon byed pa/_/bsod nams rgya mtsho dge zhing dag pa'i mtshan/_/mtha' dang dbus [{]myed [,]med [}]tshad [{]myed [,]med [}]pa'i/_/phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten sangs rgyas kun kyang mthong bar 'gyur/_/dran 
[\[323b.2\]]pa'i mthu dang stobs kyis kyang /_/de dag chos kyi sprin kyang yongs su 'dzin par 'gyur/_/de ltar khyod kyis phyogs bcu yi/_/sangs rgyas zhing rnams kun tu rgyal ba mthong nas kyang /_/byang chub spyod pa'i don gyi phyir/_/de dag kun la smon lam rgya mtsho 
[\[323b.3\]]dag byed 'gyur/_/'jig rten gtso la slob byed cing /_/gang rnams sangs rgyas sa la gnas ni bcas byas shing /_/'di tshul rgya mtshor zhugs gyur pa/_/de dag kun kyang thams cad gzigs par 'gyur/_/khyod ni 'byam klas zhing kun tu/_/bskal 
[\[323b.4\]]mang zhing rdul snyed du kun tu bzang po yi/_/spyod pa'i dam pa spyod byas shing /_/byang chub dam pa zhi ba 'thob par 'gyur/_/zhing rnams mtha' dang dbus med pa/_/ma lus kun tu bskal pa rgya mtshor spyad par gyis/_/kun tu bzang po'i spyod mchog phyir/_/
[\[323b.5\]]smon lam rgya chen yongs su rdzogs par byos/_/khyod ni smon lam thos gyur nas/_/sems can khrag khrig dga' ba yongs su skyes gyur cing /_/kun tu bzang po'i ye shes kyis/_/byang chub mchog tu smon pa byed la ltos/_/de nas 'jam dpal 
[\[323b.6\]]gzhon nur gyur pas tshigs su bcad pa de dag smras nas/_tshong dpon gyi bu nor [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]la 'di skad ces bsgo'o/_/rigs kyi bu de ltar khyod bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed cing /_/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa yongs 
[\[323b.7\]]su btsal bar sems pa legs so legs so/_/rigs kyi bu 'di ltar bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed pa de lta bu'i sems can yang rab tu dkon te/_de bas kyang gang gis bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang 
[\[324a\]]
[\[324a.1\]]chub tu sems bskyed nas/_byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa yongs su tshol ba de lta bu'i sems can ni ches dkon no/_rigs kyi bu de bas na byang chub sems dpas thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes thob par bya ba'i phyir/_yang dag 
[\[324a.2\]]pa'i dge ba'i bshes gnyen las gdon mi za bar rtogs pa thob par gyis shig_/dge ba'i bshes gnyen yongs su btsal ba la yongs su skyo ba'i yid med par gyis shig_/dge ba'i bshes gnyen blta ba la ngoms pa med par gyis shig_/dge ba'i 
[\[324a.3\]]bshes gnyen gyis bstan pa la mthun par 'dzin par gyis shig_/dge ba'i bshes gnyen gyi thabs mkhas pa'i spyod pa la khong khro ba med par gyis shig_/rigs kyi bu lho phyogs kyi rgyud 'di nyid na/_yul mi mo gya nom mchog ces bya ba yod de/_de na 
[\[324a.4\]]mgul legs pa zhes bya ba'i ri zhig yod pa de la/_dge slong sprin gyi dpal zhes bya ba 'dug gis de'i gan du song la/_/ji ltar byang chub sems dpas byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la bslab par bya ba dang /_ji ltar nan tan du bya ba dang /_ji 
[\[324a.5\]]ltar byang chub sems dpas byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa brtsam par bya ba dang /_ji ltar byang chub sems dpas byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la spyad par bya ba dang /_ji ltar byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /_ji ltar yongs 
[\[324a.6\]]su dag par bya ba dang /_ji ltar 'jug par bya ba dang /_ji ltar mngon par bsgrub par bya ba dang /_ji ltar rjes su 'brang bar bya ba dang /_ji ltar lhag par dmigs par bya ba dang /_ji ltar rgyas par bya ba dang /_ji ltar byang chub sems dpas kun tu bzang po'i 
[\[324a.7\]]spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ba dris shig dang dge ba'i bshes gnyen des khyod la kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor ston to/_/de nas tshang dpon gyi bu nor [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dga' rangs te/_mgu zhing nyams bde la tshim zhing yid bde ba 
[\[324b\]]
[\[324b.1\]]skyes nas/_'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa'i rkang pa gnyis la spyi bos phyag 'tshal te/_'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa la lan brgya stong du mar g.yas phyogs su bskor ba byas nas/_lan brgya stong du mar phyir bltas te/_dge ba'i bshes gnyen yid du 
[\[324b.2\]]'ong ba'i sems dang ldan pas/_dge ba'i bshes gnyen mi mthong ba mi bzod de/_gdong mchi mas gang bar du zhing 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa'i drung nas song ngo /_/de nas tshong dpon gyi bu nor [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]mthar gyis yul mi mo gya nom mchog ces bya ba der 
[\[324b.3\]]song ste phyin nas/_yul mi mo gya nom mchog nas rnam par brgyus te/_sngon gyi dge ba'i rtsa bas byung zhing las rgya chen pos byin gyis brlabs pa'i longs spyod yid du 'ong ba la yongs su spyod cing ri mgul legs pa gang na ba der phyin nas/_mgul legs pa'i 
[\[324b.4\]]ri la 'dzegs te/_dge slong sprin gyi dpal la chos tshol zhing /_shar phyogs logs su byung ngo /_/de bzhin du lho dang nub dang byang dang byang shar gyi phyogs mtshams dang /_shar lho'i phyogs mtshams dang /_lho nub kyi phyogs mtshams dang /_nub byang gi 
[\[324b.5\]]phyogs mtshams su yang byung ste/_bla 'og tu 'ang lta zhing dge slong sprin gyi dpal tshol tshol ba las des zhag bdun lon pa dang /_dge slong sprin gyi dpal ri rtse cig gi sman ljongs na 'chag cing 'dug pa mthong nas/_de dge slong sprin gyi dpal gang na ba der song 
[\[324b.6\]]ste/_dge slong sprin gyi dpal gyi rkang pa gnyis la spyi bos phyag 'tshal nas/_dge slong sprin gyi dpal la g.yas phyogs su bskor ba byas te/_mdun du 'dug nas thal mo sbyar te 'di skad ces smras so/_/'phags pa thugs su chud par mdzad du 
[\[324b.7\]]gsol/_bdag gis bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed na/_ji ltar byang chub sems dpas byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la bslab par bgyi ba dang /_ji ltar nan tan du bgyi ba dang /_ji ltar byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa 
[\[325a\]]
[\[325a.1\]]brtsam pa dang /_ji ltar byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la spyad par bgyi ba dang /_ji ltar byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa yongs su rdzogs par bgyi ba dang /_ji ltar yongs su dag par bgyi ba dang /_ji ltar 'jug par bgyi ba dang /_ji ltar rjes su 'brang bar bgyi ba dang /_
[\[325a.2\]]ji ltar mngon par bsgrub par bgyi ba dang /_ji ltar lhag par dmigs par bgyi ba dang /_ji ltar rgyas par bgyi ba dang /_ji ltar byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor yongs su rdzogs par 
[\[325a.3\]]'gyur ba ma 'tshal te/_'phags pa khyod ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams la gdams ngag dang /_rjes su ston pa mdzad ces thos na/_'phags pa ji ltar byang chub sems dpa' bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu 'byung bar 'gyur ba 
[\[325a.4\]]bdag la bstan du gsol/_de skad ces smras pa dang /_dge slong sprin gyi dpal gyis tshong dpon gyi bu nor [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]la 'di skad ces smras so/_/rigs kyi bu de ltar khyod kyis bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed cing /_byang chub sems 
[\[325a.5\]]dpa'i spyod pa yongs su 'dri ba legs so legs so/_/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste/_byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa yongs su btsal ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul yongs su btsal ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i 'byung ba rnam par dag pa 
[\[325a.6\]]yongs su btsal ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i lam rnam par dag pas yongs su btsal ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa rgya chen po rnam par dag pa yongs su btsal ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i mngon par shes pa bsgrub pa rnam par dag 
[\[325a.7\]]pa yongs su btsal ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa yongs su bstan pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i snang ba'i snying rje'i rgyud yongs su bstan pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i 'gro ba'i bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du rjes su 'brang ba 
[\[325b\]]
[\[325b.1\]]dang /_byang chub sems dpas 'khor ba dang /_mya ngan las 'das pa'i sgo yongs su bstan pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi 'dus byas dang /_'dus ma byas kyi skyon dang /_'jigs pas mi gos pa'i rgyud yongs su btsal ba 'di ni dka'o/_/
[\[325b.2\]]shin tu dka'o/_/rigs kyi bu kho bo ni mos pa'i stobs kyi dbang gis mig gi blo gros dang /_dad pa'i mig rnam par dag pas kun tu mngon par lta ba'i ye shes kyi 'od snang ba/_phyin phyogs med pa dang /_yul kun la thogs pa med cing sgrib pa thams cad dang 
[\[325b.3\]]bral ba'i lhag mthong la mkhas pa dang /_mig gi yul kun nas yongs su dag pa'i lus rnam par dag pa dang /_phyogs kyi rgyud 'byam klas pa thams cad du mngon par 'dud pa'i lus kyis 'dud pa la mkhas pa dang /_sangs rgyas thams cad kyi chos kyi sprin 
[\[325b.4\]]yongs su 'dzin pa'i gzungs kyi stobs kyis phyogs kyi rgyud thams cad nas mngon du gzigs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa rnams mthong ste/_'di ltar shar phyogs kyi de bzhin gshegs pa gcig kyang mthong ngo /_/sangs rgyas gnyis dang bcu dang /_sangs rgyas brgya 
[\[325b.5\]]dang /_sangs rgyas stong dang /_sangs rgyas brgya phrag stong dang /_sangs rgyas bye ba dang /_sangs rgyas bye ba brgya dang /_sangs rgyas bye ba stong dang /_sangs rgyas bye ba brgya phrag stong dang /_sangs rgyas bye ba khrag khrig brgya phrag stong nas de bzhin gshegs pa tshad med pa/_
[\[325b.6\]]dpag tu med pa/_grangs med pa/_bsam gyis mi khyab pa mtshungs pa med pa/_kun tu mtha' yas pas mtshams med pa/_gzhal du med pa/_brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa dag kyang mthong ngo /_/de bzhin gshegs pa 'dzam bu'i gling gi rdul 
[\[325b.7\]]shin tu phra ba snyed kyang mthong ngo /_/de bzhin gshegs pa gling bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rdul shin tu phra ba bsnyed dang /_stong dang /_stong gnyis pa dang /_stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyang mthong ngo /_de bzhin gshegs 
[\[326a\]]
[\[326a.1\]]pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyang mthong ngo /_/de bzhin gshegs pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing brgya'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing stong gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing brgya phrag stong 
[\[326a.2\]]gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing bye ba'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing bye ba phrag brgya'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing bye ba phrag stong gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing bye ba brgya phrag stong gi rdul shin 
[\[326a.3\]]tu phra ba snyed dang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing bye ba khrag khrig brgya phrag stong gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed nas/_sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dag kyang mthong ste/_shar phyogs logs su ji lta ba bzhin du/_lho phyogs dang nub phyogs 
[\[326a.4\]]dang /_byang phyogs dang /_byang shar gyi phyogs mtshams dang /_shar lho'i phyogs mtshams dang /_lho nub kyi phyogs mtshams dang /_nub byang gi phyogs mtshams dang /_'og dang bla'i phyogs kyi de bzhin gshegs pa gcig kyang mthong ba nas/_de bzhin gshegs 
[\[326a.5\]]pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dag kyang mthong ba'i bar du ste/_phyogs re rer rnam par bltas kyang /_de bzhin gshegs pa kha dog sna tshogs mnga' ba dang /_dbyibs sna tshogs mnga' ba dang /_rnam par 
[\[326a.6\]]'phrul pa sna tshogs mnga' ba dang /_khyu mchog gi mthu dang /_rnam par rol pa sna tshogs mnga' ba dang /_'khor gyi dkyil 'khor gyi rgyan rnam pa sna tshogs mnga' ba dang /_'od zer gyi dra ba snang ba kha dog du ma can dang /_kha dog mtha' yas pa rab tu 
[\[326a.7\]]'gyed pa dang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam par dag pa dang /_pho brang dang rgyan sna tshogs mnga' ba dang /_sku tshe'i tshad rnam par dag pa rnam pa sna tshogs mnga' ba dang /_'gro ba'i bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du rnam par dmigs par mdzad pa dang /_
[\[326b\]]
[\[326b.1\]]mngon par 'tshang rgya ba rnam par dag pa'i sgos rnam par 'phrul pa rnam pa sna tshogs mnga' ba dang /_sangs rgyas kyi khyu mchog dang seng ge'i nga ro rnam par sgrog pa mang po mthong ngo /_/de ltar rigs kyi bu kho bos ni sgo kun nas dmigs pa thams cad rnam par rig pa 
[\[326b.2\]]la yang dag par gzhol ba'i sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa 'di thob par zad na/_byang chub sems dpa' ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor rnam par dag pa gang gis/_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dkyil 'khor dang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing dang pho brang rnam par dag 
[\[326b.3\]]pa'i rgyan mngon par mthong bas/_sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo kun nas snang ba'i dkyil 'khor rab tu thob pa dang /_bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du rnam par dmigs par de bzhin gshegs pa mthong ba rnam par dag pas/_sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo 
[\[326b.4\]]'gro ba thams cad du sgro btags pa rab tu thob pa dang /_gang gis de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu tshad med pa'i rjes su 'brang bas sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo/_stobs kyis sgro btags pa rab tu thob pa dang /_gang gis chos mnyan pa'i rnam pas/_
[\[326b.5\]]de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku'i sprin la lta bas/_sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo chos kyis sgro btags pa rab tu thob pa dang gang gis phyogs rgya mtsho thams cad kyi sangs rgyas rgya mtsho tha mi dad pa la 'jug pas/_sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i 
[\[326b.6\]]sgo phyogs rnam par snang ba'i snying po rab tu thob pa dang /_gang gis de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa dang /_khyu mchog gi mthu phyung ba la rab tu phra bar dmigs shing 'jug pas/_sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo phyogs thams cad du 'jug 
[\[326b.7\]]pa rab tu thob pa dang /_bskal pa thams cad du de bzhin gshegs pa mthong zhing rnam par dmigs pa dang mi bral bas/_sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo bskal par sgro btags pa rab tu thob pa dang /_gang gis dus thams cad du de bzhin gshegs pa mthong 
[\[327a\]]
[\[327a.1\]]zhing 'grogs pa dang mi 'bral bas/_sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo dus su sgro btags pa rab tu thob pa dang /_gang gis sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad nas mngon par 'phags shing zil gyis mi non pa'i sangs rgyas kyi sku yongs su 
[\[327a.2\]]mthong zhing rnam par rig pa'i sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo zhing du sgro btags pa rab tu thob pa dang /_gang gis dus gsum gyis de bzhin gshegs pa'i dkyil 'khor rang gi sems dang bsam par yang dag par gzhol bas/_/sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo dus 
[\[327a.3\]]gsum du sgro btags pa rab tu thob pa dang /_gang gis dmigs pa thams cad la de bzhin gshegs pa gcig nas gcig tu brgyud pa yang dag par tshogs pa mthong zhing dmigs pas sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo dmigs par sgro btags pa rab tu thob pa dang /_gang gis skad 
[\[327a.4\]]cig gcig la 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba rnam par dmigs pas/_sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo zhi bas sgro btags pa rab tu thob pa dang /_gang gis nyin gcig tu gnas 
[\[327a.5\]]thams cad du de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad gshegs par rnam par dmigs pas/_sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo 'bral bas sgro btags pa rab tu thob pa dang /_gang gis de bzhin gshegs pa re re skyil mo krung du bzhugs pas kyang /_
[\[327a.6\]]chos kyi dbyings thams cad du rgyas par khyab pa'i sangs rgyas kyi sku rnam par dmigs pas/_sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo shin tu yangs pas sgro btags pa rab tu thob pa dang /_gang gis skra'i rtse mo gcig la sangs rgyas brjod du med 
[\[327a.7\]]pa byung ba mnyes par bya ba la 'jug pas/_sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo shin tu phra bas sgro btags pa rab tu thob pa dang /_skad cig gcig la 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du mngon par 'tshang rgya zhing /_rnam par 'phrul pa ston pa rnam par dmigs pas/_
[\[327b\]]
[\[327b.1\]]gang gis sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo rgyan du sgro btags pa rab tu thob pa dang /_sangs rgyas byung ba thams cad kyi chos kyi 'khor lo dang /_rnam par 'phrul pa dang /_ye shes snang ba rab tu thob pas gang gis sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo bgos par 
[\[327b.2\]]sgro btags pa rab tu thob pa dang /_rang gis sems dang bsam pa la bltas nas/_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad snang ba rab tu thob pas/_sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo sems kyis sgro btags pa rab tu thob pa dang /_'gro ba thams cad 
[\[327b.3\]]kyis las rnam pa ji lta bur bstsags pa'i gzugs brnyan yongs su ston pas/_gang gis sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo las kyis sgro btags pa rab tu thob pa dang /_chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad pad mo'i tshal gyis rgyas par 
[\[327b.4\]]sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul pa rgya chen po ston pa/_phyogs kun tu mngon sum du dmigs pas/_gang gis sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo rnam par 'phrul pas sgro btags pa rab tu thob pa dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku gzugs brnyan gyi sprin 
[\[327b.5\]]dbyangs sgrogs pas chos kyi dbyings dang nam mkha' kun nas snang ba/_gang gis sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i sgo nam mkhas sgro btags pa rab tu thob pa de dag gi spyod pa shes pa'am/_yon tan brjod par nus re skan/_song rigs kyi bu lho phyogs kyi rgyud 
[\[327b.6\]]'di nyid na phyogs kyi ljongs rgya mtsho'i sgo zhes bya ba yod pa de na/_dge slong rgya mtsho'i sprin ces bya ba 'dug gi_/de'i gan du song la ji ltar byang chub sems dpas byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la bslab par bya ba dang /_ji ltar nan tan du bya ba dris shig 
[\[327b.7\]]dang /_rigs kyi bu dge ba'i bshes gnyen de khyod la dge ba'i rtsa ba'i tshogs dang /_rgyu yongs su ston par 'gyur ro/_/tshogs kyis rgya chen po yang khong du chud par byed do/_/dge ba'i rtsa ba'i stobs dang /_shugs rgya chen po yang yongs su skyed 
[\[328a\]]
[\[328a.1\]]par byed do/_/byang chub kyi sems kyi tshogs dang rgyu rgya chen po yang yongs su brjod par 'gyur ro/_/theg pa chen po rab tu thob pa dang /_snang bar 'gyur ba rgya chen po yang bskyed par byed do/_/pha rol tu phyin pa'i tshogs kyi stobs rgya chen 
[\[328a.2\]]po yang rab tu ston par byed do/_/spyod pa rgya mtsho la 'jug pa'i tshul rgya chen po yang rab tu rjod par 'gyur ro/_/smon lam gyi dkyil 'khor rgya chen po yang yongs su sbyong bar 'gyur ro/_/sgo kun nas 'byung ba'i rgyan rgya chen po yang rnam par sbyong bar 'gyur 
[\[328a.3\]]ro/_/snying rje chen po'i stobs rgya chen po yang yongs su 'phel bar byed do/_/de nas tshong dpon gyi bu nor [{]bzangs kyis [,]bzang gis [}]dge slong sprin gyi dpal gyi rkang pa gnyis la spyi bos phyag 'tshal nas/_dge slong sprin gyi dpal la lan brgya stong du mar g.yas phyogs 
[\[328a.4\]]su bskor ba byas te/_lan brgya stong du mar phyir bltas nas/_dge slong sprin gyi dpal gyi drung nas song ngo //_//sangs rgyas rmad gcad ces bya ba shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo/_/bam po nyi shu brgyad pa/_/de nas tshong dpon gyi bu nor 
[\[328a.5\]{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dge ba'i bshes gnyen gyi gdams ngag de bzhin rjes su bsam pa dang /_ye shes snang ba de rjes su dran par bya ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa de rnam par dpyad pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi tshul de rjes su 
[\[328a.6\]]sbyang ba dang byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho'i tshul de blta ba dang /_sangs rgyas kyi dkyil 'khor de la mngon par mos par bya ba dang /_sangs rgyas blta ba'i phyogs de la mngon par spro bar bya ba dang /_sangs rgyas rgya mtsho de la rjes su bsam pa dang /_sangs rgyas 
[\[328a.7\]]gcig nas gcig tu brgyud pa de rjes su dran par bya ba dang /_sangs rgyas kyi tshul rtogs par bya ba de khong du chud par bya ba dang /_sangs rgyas kyi nam mkha' de la rjes su blta bar byed cing /_mthar gyis phyogs kyis ljongs rgya mtsho'i sgo gang na ba dang /_
[\[328b\]]
[\[328b.1\]]dge slong rgya mtsho'i sprin gang na ba der phyin nas/_dge slong rgya mtsho'i sprin gyi rkang pa gnyis la spyi bos phyag 'tshal te/_dge slong rgya mtsho'i sprin la lan brgya stong du mar g.yas phyogs su bskor ba byas nas/_dge slong rgya mtsho'i sprin gyi mdun du 'dug ste thal mo sbyar 
[\[328b.2\]]nas 'di skad ces smras so/_/'phags pa bdag ni bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par zhugs te/_bla na med pa'i ye shes rgya mtsho la 'jug par 'tshal na/_ji ltar byang chub sems dpa' rnams 'jig rten gyi rgyud 
[\[328b.3\]]las phyir ldog cing de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs su 'grub par 'gyur ba dang /_'khor ba'i rgya mtsho las rgal zhing thams cad mkhyen pa'i rgya mtshor 'jug par 'gyur ba dang /_byis pa so so'i skye bo'i sa las gyen du 'phags par 'gyur zhing /_
[\[328b.4\]]de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs su 'grub par 'gyur ba dang /_'khor ba'i rgyud las ldog par 'gyur zhing /_byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa'i rgyud du 'jug par 'gyur ba dang /_'khor ba'i rgya mtsho rgyud kyi 'khor lo las phyir ldog cing byang chub sems dpa'i 
[\[328b.5\]]spyod pa dang /_smon lam gyi 'khor lor 'jug par 'gyur ba dang /_bdud kyi dkyil 'khor thams cad rab tu 'joms shing sangs rgyas kyi dkyil 'khor 'byung ba thams cad rab tu ston pa dang /_srid pa'i rgya mtsho yongs su skems shing snying rje chen po'i mtsho 
[\[328b.6\]]rnam par 'phel bar byed pa dang /_mi khom pa dang ngan song dang /_ngan 'gro dang log par ltung ba thams cad kyi sgo gcod cing /_mtho ris dang mya ngan las 'das pa'i sgo 'byed pa dang /_khams gsum gyi grong khyer gyi sgo rnam par gcod cing thams cad mkhyen pa 
[\[328b.7\]]nyid kyi pho brang gi sgo rnam par 'byed pa dang /_yo byad thams cad kyi sred pa rnam par spong zhing 'gro ba thams cad bsdu ba'i smon lam yongs su bskyed par 'gyur ba ma 'tshal lo/_/de skad ces smras pa dang /_dge slong rgya mtsho'i sprin gyis tshong dpon gyi bu nor 
[\[329a\]]
[\[329a.1\]{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]la 'di skad ces smras so/_/rigs kyi bu khyod kyis de ltar bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed pa legs so legs so/_/rigs kyi bu dge ba'i rtsa ba ma bskyed pa'i sems can rnams ni byang chub tu 
[\[329a.2\]]sems kyang mi skye ba'i dge ba'i rtsa ba sgo kun tu snang ba rab tu thob pa dang /_thabs kyi snying po dang ldan pa'i lam gyi ting nge 'dzin gyi ye shes snang bas rab tu snang bar 'gyur ba dang /_bsod nams kyi rgya mtsho'i tshogs rgya chen po bstsags pa dang /_dkar 
[\[329a.3\]]po'i tshogs thams cad kyi rgyun mi 'chad pa dang /_dge ba'i bshes gnyen thams cad bkur bas yongs su skyo ba med pa dang /_lus dang srog la 'phangs pa mi rtsi ba dang /_dngos por 'dzin pa thams cad dang bral ba dang /_mthon dman med pa'i sa 
[\[329a.4\]]ltar sems mnyam pa dang /_rang bzhin gyi snying rje che zhing byams par sems pa dang ldan pa dang /_srid pa'i lam gyi rgyud thams cad du gnas par 'dug pa dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul la mngon par mos pa'i sems can rnams byang chub tu 
[\[329a.5\]]sems skye ste/_'di ltar sems can thams cad yongs su bskyab pa'i phyir snying rje chen po'i sems dang 'gro ba thams cad la snyoms par nan tan du bya ba'i phyir byams pa chen po'i sems dang /_'gro ba thams cad kyi sdug bsngal gyi phung po zhi bar bya ba'i phyir bde 
[\[329a.6\]]ba'i sems dang /_mi dge ba'i chos thams cad las rnam par bzlog pa'i phyir phan pa'i sems dang /_'jigs pa thams cad las yongs su bsrung ba'i phyir snying brtse ba'i sems dang /_bsgribs pa thams cad rnam par bzlog pa'i phyir chags pa med pa'i 
[\[329a.7\]]sems dang /_chos kyi dbyings thams cad rgyas par dgang ba'i phyir/_rgya chen po'i sems dang nam mkha'i dbyings kun tu gzhol zhing kun tu 'gro bar bya ba'i phyir mtha' yas pa'i sems dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mthong zhing rnam par 
[\[329b\]]
[\[329b.1\]]dmigs par bya ba'i phyir dri ma med pa'i sems dang /_dus gsum gyi dmigs pa ma lus par ye shes kyis rgyas par dgang ba'i phyir rnam par dag pa'i sems dang /_thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes rgya mtsho la 'jug par bya ba'i phyir ye shes kyi sems kyang skye

[\[329b.3\]]dpag dka' ba dang /_bags kyis gzhol zhing legs par gnas pa dang /_rin po che'i 'byung gnas du mas rnam par brgyan pa dang /_chu'i phung po tshad med pa dang /_kha dog rnam pa tha dad pa mang zhing bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang /_'byung po mtha' yas

[\[329b.6\]]la brtson par gnas pa'i tshe/_rgya mtsho chen po'i 'og gi gzhi las pad mo chen po nor bu rin po che in+d+ra nI la thub pa med cing rdo rje lta bur sra ba'i chu ba can/_nor bu rin po che baiDUR+Ya dam pa'i rgyan dang ldan pa/_'dzam bu chu klung gi gser ltar dri ma med pa'i 
[\[329b.7\]]lo ma rgya chen po can/_dus dang mthun pa'i tsan dan gyi snying po'i rgyan gyis brgyan pa/_rin po che a sma gar b+ha'i ze ba can/_rgya mtsho tsam du yangs shing rgya che ba lha ma yin gyi dbang po brgya stong phrag bcus sdong po'i lhun nas bzung ba/_rin po che sna tshogs brgya 
[\[330a\]]
[\[330a.1\]]phrag stong gi dra bas kun nas yog pa/_klu yi dbang po brgya stong phrag bcus spos chu'i char mngon par phab pa/_nam mkha' lding gi dbang po brgya stong phrag bcus dar dang rin po che'i lda ldi dang se mo do mang po mchus bzung zhing rab tu spyangs

[\[330a.3\]]bcus rol mo dang glu sna tshogs snyan pas bstod cing mchod pa/_lha'i dbang po brgya stong phrag bcus lha rdzas kyi me tog dang /_spos dang phreng ba dang /_bdug spos dang /_byug pa dang /_phye ma dang /_gos dang /_gdugs dang /_rgyal mtshan dang /_lha'i ba dan gyi sprin

[\[330a.5\]]phrag bcus rin po che bdun gyis bsu zhing mngon par mchod pa/_rgya mtsho'i lha brgya stong phrag bcus mngon par mthon cing phyag bya ba/_nor bu rin po che skar ma'i dad brgya stong phrag bcus 'od zer gyi rgyan gyis rab tu snang bar byas pa/_nor bu rin po che bsod

[\[357a.1\]]grangs med pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed byung nas/_sangs rgyas thams cad kyi thabs mkhas pa'i tshul gyi dkyil 'khor rab tu brjod pa byed cing ba spu'i bu ga thams cad nas thabs mkhas pa dang ldan pa'i sngon gyi sbyor ba mang 
[\[357a.2\]]po'i sprin rab tu dgye ba dang /_'jig rten rnams la thabs mkhas pa'i spyod pa rab tu brjod pa dang /_theg pa chen po'i 'byung ba mngon par bstan pa dang /_sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dkyil 'khor yang dag par bsngags pa dang /_'khor ba 
[\[357a.3\]]dang mya ngan las 'das pa tha [{]myi [,]mi [}]dad pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa yongs su bstan pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi thabs mkhas pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la sems can rab tu dgod pa dang /_'jig rten rnams la byang chub sems

[\[357b.1\]]la chos kyi dbyings rgyas par 'gengs pa mthong ngo //_//sangs rgyas rmad gcad ces bya ba shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo/_/bam po sum cu pa/_yang byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi zhing grangs med pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed 
[\[357b.2\]]byung ste/_byang chub sems dpa'i stobs rab tu snang bar bya ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i stobs yongs su bsgrub pa'i sgra rab tu dbyung ba dang /_ba spu'i bu ga thams cad nas stobs kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i sgrub cing 'byin pa'i sngon gyi sbyor ba mang po'i

[\[357b.6\]]ba spu'i bu ga thams cad nas ye shes kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su dag pa dang ldan pa'i sngon gyi sbyor ba mang po'i sprin rab tu dgye ba dang /_'jig rten rnams la sangs rgyas thams cad kyi yon tan dang /_ye shes mngon par shes pa dang ldan pa'i ye shes

[\[358b.1\]]rnams la chos kyi dbyings kyi gzhi'i bye brag mngon sum du shes pa'i sprin ces bya ba'i chos kyi sprin gyi char mngon par dbab pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa' dbang bskur ba thob pa rnams la gzhi kun tu rten pa'i sprin ces bya ba'i chos kyi sprin gyi char mngon par dbab pa

[\[382b.1\]]bdud stong phrag bcu yang steng gi bar snang la 'khod nas/_lha rdzas kyi nor bu rin po ches mngon par gtor te/_'di skad ces 'dzer to/_/rigs kyi bu 'phags pa 'di dka' thub lngas yongs su gdung ba mdzad pa'i tshe/_
[\[382b.2\]]'od 'phro ba'i phung po 'di las snang ba chen po byung zhing kho bo cag gi khyim dang /_lus dang rgyan gyi longs spyod thams cad zil gyis gnon to/_/de'i phyir kho bo cag kyang so so'i gnas gnas na bag dga' ba med pas 'khor dang bcas te 
[\[382b.3\]]'phags pa 'di'i drung du 'dong ngo /_/kho bo cag 'di'i drung du phyin nas kyang /_'phags pa 'di ni kho bo cag la ci nas kyang rang rang gi sems sor chud nas byang chub tu sems bskyed de/_bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang 
[\[382b.4\]]chub las phyir mi ldog par 'gyur ba de lta de ltar chos ston to/_dbang bsgyur gyi lha'i rgyal po/_stong phrag bcus kyang lha rdzas kyi me tog gis mngon par gtor te/_'di skad ces 'dzer to/_/rigs kyi bu 'phags pa 'di dka' 
[\[382b.5\]]thub lngas yongs su gdung ba byed pa'i tshe/_kho bo cag kyang rang rang gi gnas na dga' ba med de/_de ltar bdag cag kyang rang rang gi skye bo'i 'khor dang bcas te 'di'i drung du 'dong ngo /_/bdag cag 'di'i drung du phyin pa dang 'di ni bdag 
[\[382b.6\]]cag la rang rang gi sems la dbang bsgyur ba thob par bya ba'i phyir chos ston to/_/nyon mongs pa thams cad la dbang bsgyur ba thob par bya ba dang /_bsam pa ji lta bzhin du skye ba la dbang bsgyur ba thob par bya ba dang /_las kyi sgrib 
[\[382b.7\]]pa thams cad rnam par dag pa'i dbang rab tu thob par bya ba dang /_snyoms par 'jug pa thams cad la dbang rab tu thob par bya ba dang /_yo byad la dbang ba'i rgyan yongs su dag par bya ba dang /_bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du 
[\[383a\]]
[\[383a.1\]]tshe la dbang bsgyur ba thob par bya ba'i phyir chos ston to/_/rab 'phrul dga'i lha'i rgyal po stong phrag bcu yang steng gi bar snang la 'khod de/_lha rdzas kyi glu yang dag par sbyar ba'i nga ro snyan pa'i dbyangs bsgrags 
[\[383a.2\]]te/_mchod pa byas nas 'di skad ces 'dzer to/_/rigs kyi bu 'phags pa 'di dka' thub lngas yongs su gdung ba mdzad pa'i tshe/_me'i phung po 'di las de dang 'dra ba'i 'od byung zhing des kho bo cag gi gzhal med khang 'di dag 
[\[383a.3\]]rnam par dag ste/_gsal ba dang 'od shin tu dang bar gyur to/_/rgyan 'di dang lha'i bu mo 'di dag kyang rnam par dag cing gsal la 'od shin du dang bar gyur to/_/de bas na bdag cag kyang lha'i bu dang lha mo'i tshogs kyi 'khor dang 
[\[383a.4\]]bcas par 'dod pa la dga' bar tshor ba med do/_/'dod pa'i bde ba la mngon par dga' ba med pas lus dang sems tshim par gyur nas/_'phags pa 'di'i drung du dong ba yin te/_'phags pa 'di ni bdag cag de ltar phyin pa rnams la 
[\[383a.5\]]sems rnam par dag par bya ba'i phyir chos ston to/_/sems kyi 'od 'dang bar bya ba dang /_sems shin du dag par bya ba dang /_sems las su rung bar bya ba dang /_sems kyi dga' ba yongs su bskyed pa dang /_stobs bcu'i ye shes 
[\[383a.6\]]'thob pa rnam par dag par bya ba dang /_chos kyi shugs chen po rnam par 'phel bar bya ba dang /_sangs rgyas kyi sku tshad med pa mngon par bsgrub pa'i phyir lus rnam par dag par bya ba dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung thob par bya ba'i phyir 
[\[383a.7\]]ngag rnam par dag par bya ba dang /_thams cad mkhyen pa nyid rab tu thob par bya bar sems rnam par dag par bya ba'i phyir chos ston to/_/rab dga' ldan gyi lha'i rgyal po stong phrag bcu'i lha'i bu dang /_lha'i bu mo'i tshogs kyi 'khor dang bcas 
[\[383b\]]
[\[383b.1\]]pa dag kyang steng gi bar snang la 'khod nas/_spos sna tshogs kyi phye ma'i sprin gyi char mngon par phab pas mchod pa byas te/_phyag 'tshal nas 'di skad ces 'dzer to/_/rigs kyi bu 'phags pa 'di dka' thub lngas yongs su gdung ba mdzad pa'i 
[\[383b.2\]]tshe/_bdag cag kyang rang rang gi gnas na dga' ba med de/_de ltar dga' ba med pas 'di'i drung du 'dong ste/_bdag cag phyin pa rnams la 'phags pa 'di ni yul thams cad la ltos pa med par bya ba'i phyir chos ston to/_/chog shes 
[\[383b.3\]]pa'i sems su bya ba dang /_dge ba'i rtsa ba yang dag par bskyed pa dang /_byang chub tu sems bskyed pa rab tu thob par bya ba nas/_sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'i phyir chos ston to/_/rab mtshe ma'i lha'i 
[\[383b.4\]]rgyal po stong phrag bcu lha'i bu dang /_lha'i bu mo'i tshogs kyi 'khor dang bcas pa dag gis kyang lha rdzas kyi me tog man da ra ba'i char mngon par phab ste/_'di skad ces 'dzer to/_/rigs kyi bu 'phags pa 'di dka' thub lngas 
[\[383b.5\]]yongs su gdung ba mdzad pa'i tshe/_bdag cag kyang lha rdzas kyi glu snyan pa la dga' ba med de/_de ltar dga' ba med pas 'phags pa 'di'i drung du 'dong ste/_bdag cag phyin nas kyang /_'phags pa 'di ni 'dod pa'i dga' 
[\[383b.6\]]ba thams cad rnam par bzlog par bya ba nas/_sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad rab tu thob par bya ba'i phyir chos ston to/_/lha'i dbang po brgya byin stong phrag bcu so so nas/_nye ba'i dbang po sum cu rtsa gnyis dang /_
[\[383b.7\]]lha'i bu dang /_lha'i bu mo'i tshogs kyi 'khor dang bcas pas/_lha rdzas kyi gos dang /_rin po che'i rgyan dang /_me tog gi sprin gyi char mngon par phab ste/_'di skad ces 'dzer to/_/rigs kyi bu 
[\[384a\]]
[\[384a.1\]]'phags pa 'di/_dka' thub lngas yongs su gdung ba mdzad pa'i tshe/_bdag cag kyang brgya byin gyi khang pa dang /_bza' shing gi ra ba dang /_rtse ba'i nags tshal dang /_lha rdzas kyi rol mo 
[\[384a.2\]]brdung zhing dkrol ba dang /_gam yo na glu len pa'i longs spyod la dga' ba med de/_de ltar bdag cag dga' ba med pas 'di'i drung du 'dong ngo /_/bdag cag der phyin nas kyang 'phags pa 'di ni kho bo cag la 'dod pa'i dga' ba thams 
[\[384a.3\]]cad yongs su spang ba'i phyir chos ston to/_/'di dag thams cad ni rtag pa ma yin te/_mi brtan zhing 'jig par 'gyur ba'i chos can no zhes bya ba'i tshig brjod do/_/dregs pa dang bag med pa thams cad yongs su gcad pa dang /_bla na 
[\[384a.4\]]med pa'i byang chub tu spro ba bskyed pa'i phyir chos ston to/_/rigs kyi bu gzhan yang 'dis bltas pa tsam gyis ri rab kyi rtse mo thams cad kyang g.yo bar 'gyur te/_des bdag cag gshigs pas sems skyo bar gyur nas/_thams 
[\[384a.5\]]cad mkhyen pa'i sems brtan pas/_thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes su smon lam mngon par sgrub bo/_/klu'i rgyal po shugs ldan dang /_dga' bo dang /_bsnyen dga' bo la [{]stsogs [,]sogs [}]pa/_klu brgya stong phrag bcu dag kyang steng gi bar snang la 
[\[384a.6\]]gnas shing dus dang mthun pa'i lha rdzas kyi tsan dan gyi sprin dang /_klu'i bu mos glu blangs pa'i nga ro snyan pa'i dbyangs dang /_lha rdzas kyi spos chu'i rgyun rab tu 'bab pa'i char mngon par phab ste/_'di skad ces 'dzer to/_/rigs kyi bu 'phags 
[\[384a.7\]]pa 'di dka' thub lngas yongs su gdung ba mdzad pa'i tshe/_me'i phung po chen po 'di las 'od chen po byung ste/_des klu'i gnas thams cad snang bar byas nas/_bye ma'i char dang /_nam mkha' lding gi 'jigs pa yongs su sel to/_/khro ba 
[\[384b\]]
[\[384b.1\]]'byung ba yang zhi bar byas te/_lus sim par byas nas yid shin tu mos par byed do/_/de ltar sems shin tu mos par gyur nas/_'phags pa 'di ni bdag cag la chos ston te/_'di ltar klu'i lam gyi rgyud ngan pa rnam par smad pa dang /_sgrib pa'i 
[\[384b.2\]]las thams cad rab tu spang ba'i phyir nyes pa 'chags su btsud cing bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed du btsud de thams cad mkhyen pa nyid la rab tu dgod do/_/gnod sbyin gyi dbang po stong phrag bcu yang nam mkha'i dbyings 
[\[384b.3\]]la 'khod de/_mchod pa rnam pa sna tshogs kyis bram ze rgyal ba'i drod kyi skye mched dang /_tshong dpon gyi bu nor [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]la mchod pa byas nas 'di skad ces 'dzer to/_/rigs kyi bu 'phags pa 'di dka' thub lngas yongs su gdung ba mdzad pa'i tshe/_
[\[384b.4\]]kho bo cag gi 'khor kun kyang mi rnams la byams pa'i sems skye bar 'gyur te/_gnod sbyin dang /_srin po dang /_grul bum thams cad kyang byams pa'i sems dang ldan par gyur to/_/de dag byams pa'i sems dang ldan zhing 'tshe ba med 
[\[384b.5\]]pa la zhugs pa rnams kho bo cag gi drung du 'ong ngo /_/kho bo cag kyang de ltar byams pa'i dbang gi zil gyis non pas/_rang rang gi gnas de dang /_de dag na dga' ba med de/_de ltar bdag cag kyang rang rang gi skye bo'i 'khor dang bcas te/_
[\[384b.6\]]'phags pa 'di'i drung du 'dong ngo /_/'phags pa bdag cag de ltar 'ongs pa rnams la 'phags pa 'di'i sku las byung ba'i 'od kyis lus thams cad snang bar byas shing /_bde bas khyab par mdzad de/_de ltar bdag cag lus 
[\[384b.7\]]dang sems bde bar gyur nas/_'phags pa 'di ni ci nas kyang bdag cag la gnod sbyin dang /_srin po dang grul bum dang /_lus srul po mang po byang chub tu sems skye bar 'gyur ba de ltar chos ston to/_/dri za'i dbang po stong phrag 
[\[385a\]]
[\[385a.1\]]bcu dag kyang steng gi bar snang la 'khod nas 'di skad ces 'dzer to/_/rigs kyi bu kho bo cag kyang rang rang gi gnas na 'khod pa'i tshe/_'phags pa 'di dka' thub lngas yongs su gdung ba mdzad pa'i tshe/_me'i phung 
[\[385a.2\]]po 'di las 'od byung ste/_bdag cag gi gnas snang bar gyur to/_/de ltar bdag cag 'od des phog ste/_bde ba bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang ldan par gyur pas/_'phags pa 'di'i drung du 'dong ste/_bdag cag drung du phyin nas

[\[385a.5\]]'jig rten rgya mtsho dang rir bcas pa'i sa chen po'i dkyil 'khor rab tu g.yo bar gyur te/_bdag cag kun gyi nga rgyal dang rlom sems dang /_dregs pa thams cad brtul shugs dang /_dka' thub kyi zil gyis non te/_
[\[385a.6\]]'phags pa 'di'i drung du 'dong ngo /_/der phyin nas kyang 'phags pa 'di ni bdag cag la g.yo sgyu thams cad rab tu spang ba dang /_chos zab mo'i bzod pa la gzud pa dang /_mi g.yo ba'i chos nyid la rab tu dgod pa 
[\[385a.7\]]dang /_stobs bcu'i ye shes yongs su grub par bya ba'i phyir chos ston to/_/nam mkha' lding gi dbang po shugs chen po 'dzin pa la [{]stsogs [,]sogs [}]pa nam mkha' lding gi dbang po stong phrag bcus kyang bram ze'i bu shin tu sdug gu'i 
[\[385b\]]
[\[385b.1\]]gzugs su mngon par sprul te/_'di skad ces 'dzer to/_/rigs kyi bu 'phags pa 'di dka' thub lngas yongs su gdung ba mdzad pa'i tshe/_me'i phung po 'di dag las 'od chen po byung ste/_bdag cag gi gnas snang bar byas nas/_yongs

[\[385b.3\]]sbyor ba mdzad do/_/snying rje chen po la yang dag par dgod pa mdzad do/_/'khor ba'i rgya mtshor 'jug pa la yang yang dag par sbyor ba mdzad do/_/'dod pa'i 'dam du bying ba'i sems can mngon par gdon par bya ba la'ang yongs su sbyor ba mdzad 
[\[385b.4\]]do/_/lhag pa'i bsam pa'i sgo rnam par dag par bya ba la'ang rab tu sbyor ba mdzad do/_/shes rab dang thabs shin tu gsal ba la'ang yang dag par sbyor ba mdzad do/_/rnam pa ji lta bur yongs su smyin par bya ba'i sems can rnams gdul bar bya ba la'ang 
[\[385b.5\]]rab tu sbyor ba mdzad do/_/mi'am ci'i dbang po stong phrag bcu yang steng gi bar snang la 'khod de 'di skad ces 'dzer to/_/rigs kyi bu 'phags pa 'di dka' thub lngas yongs su gdung ba mdzad pa'i tshe/_kho bo cag gi gnas na 'khod pa'i 
[\[385b.6\]]shing ta la'i phreng ba las dril bu'i dra ba rin po che'i srad bus lda ldir brgyus pa'i sil snyan dang /_rol mo'i yo byad dang /_rin po che'i rgyan dang /_khyim gyi longs spyod thams cad las sangs rgyas kyi sgra byung ngo /_/chos kyi sgra dang 
[\[385b.7\]]phyir mi ldog pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i dge 'dun gyi sgra dang /_byang chub tu yang dag par zhugs pa'i smon lam gyi sgra byung ngo /_/'jig rten gyi khams kyi grangs su bya ba che ge mo zhig na ni byang chub sems dpa' [{]mying [,]ming [}]'di dang 'di zhes 
[\[386a\]]
[\[386a.1\]]bya ba zhig byang chub tu smon lam 'debs so/_/'jig rten gyi khams kyi grangs su bya ba che ge mo zhig na ni byang chub sems dpa'i [{]mying [,]ming [}]'di dang 'di zhes bya ba zhig dka' ba yongs su gtong ba byed do/_/'jig rten gyi khams kyi grangs 
[\[386a.2\]]su bya ba che ge mo zhig na ni byang chub sems dpa' [{]mying [,]ming [}]'di dang 'di zhes bya ba zhig thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor yongs su sbyong ba byed do/_/'jig rten gyi khams kyi grangs su bya ba che ge mo zhig na ni byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[386a.3\]{]mying [,]ming [}]'di dang 'di zhes bya ba zhig snying po byang chub tu 'gro'o/_/'jig rten gyi khams kyi grangs su bya ba che ge mo zhig na ni byang chub sems dpa' [{]mying [,]ming [}]'di dang 'di zhes bya ba zhig dpung dang bzhon par bcas pa'i bdud rab tu pham par byas te/_bla 
[\[386a.4\]]na [{]myed [,]med [}]pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par 'tshang rgya'o/_/'jig rten gyi khams kyi grangs su bya ba che ge mo zhig na ni de bzhin gshegs pa mtshan 'di dang 'di zhes bya ba zhig chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba mdzad do/_/'jig rten gyi 
[\[386a.5\]]khams kyi grangs su bya ba che ge mo zhig na ni de bzhin gshegs pa mtshan 'di dang ni che ge mo zhig sangs rgyas kyi 'phrin las ma lus par mdzad nas/_phung po ma lus pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa'i dbyings su yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba

[\[386b.1\]]gyi longs spyod rlung gis bskyod pa'i sgra 'byung zhing /_nga ro 'byin pa dang /_rna lam du grags pa'i mtha' ni thug par mi rung ngo /_/de ltar bdag cag kyang sangs rgyas dang /_chos dang /_dge 'dun dang /_byang chub sems dpa' rab tu 'jug

[\[387a.1\]]chub tu smon lam 'debs par 'gyur ba de ltar chos ston pa mdzad do/_/de nas tshong dpon gyi bu nor [{]bzangs kyis [,]bzang gis [}]chos kyi tshul rnam pa 'di lta bu thos nas dga' rangs te/_mgu zhing nyams bde la tshim zhing yid

[\[387b\]]
[\[387b.1\]]yongs su mi zad pa thob pa ste/_rigs kyi bu kho bos ni byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa dkyil 'khor yongs su mi zad pa 'di shes par zad na/_kho bos byang chub sems dpa' me'i phung po lta bu 'gro ba thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa 
[\[387b.2\]]dang /_lta ba'i rnam pa thams cad yongs su zad par bya ba'i smon lam can ru mtshon phyir mi ldog pa dang ldan pa/_sems la thub pa med cing sems dma' ba med pa dang ldan pa/_yid zhum pa med pa can rdo rje'i snying po dang ldan pa'i
